                                                    UNITED TO THE GREEN FUTURE
                                                         FINANCIAL AND OPERATIONAL HIGHLIGHTS
                                                                              財務及業務摘要
昆侖能源把綠色作為發展之路，推動戰略接替，加快由天然氣經銷商
    向國際知名、國內一流綠色能源綜合供應商的轉型。
   To promote green development, the Group will smooth the different business strategy,
and expedite its transformation from a natural gas distributor into an internationally renowned
                    and China’s first-class integrated green energy provider.

(1)    Net profit margin is profit for the year from continuing operations divided by            (1) 淨利潤率指持續經營業務年內溢利除以持續經營
       revenue from continuing operations.                                                           業務收入 。
(2)    Return on total assets is profit for the year from continuing operations divided          (2) 總資產回報率指持續經營業務年內溢利除以持續
       by average total assets from continuing operations.                                           經營業務平均資產總額 。
(3)    Return on capital employed is the ratio of profit before income tax expense and           (3) 淨資本回報率指持續經營業務除所得稅費用及利
       interest from continuing operations divided by total assets less current liabilities          息前溢利除以持續經營業務總資產減流動負債 。
       from continuing operations.
(4)    Liabilities-to-Adjusted EBITDA ratio is the ratio of total liabilities from continuing    (4)    負債對經調整EBITDA倍數指持續經營業務總負債
       operations divided by adjusted EBITDA.                                                           除以經調整利息 、稅項 、折舊及攤銷前盈利 。
(5)    Debts-to-net assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings          (5)    淨資產負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除以總
       and lease liabilities divided by total equity.                                                   權益 。
(6)    Gearing ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings, and lease           (6)    資本負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除以總權
       liabilities divided by the sum of total equity, interest-bearing borrowings and lease            益 、計息借貸與租賃負債之和 。
       liability.
(7)    Debts-to-assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings and          (7)    資產負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除以總資
       lease liabilities divided by total assets.                                                       產。
(8)    Average finance cost is the ratio of aggregated amount of each category of                (8)    平均融資成本指各種類借貸成本乘其佔持續經營
       borrowing multiply by its interest rate divided by the total borrowing from                      業務總借貸比例 ，然後加總 。
       continuing operations.
(9)    Due to certain Group’s businesses are classified as discontinued operations in            (9)    由於2020年本集團部份業務已分類為已終止持續
       2020. The financial information of the Group has been restated.                                  經營業務 。本集團的財務資料已重列 。
(10)   The financial information of the Group has not been restated for the potential            (10)   本集團財務資料未就有意出售已終止持續經營業
       disposal of discontinued operations as the directors consider that the unrestated                務進行重列 ，原因是董事認為未經重列的財務資
       financial information is more appropriate for year-on-year comparison of the                     料更適合同比比較本集團業務營運的變化情況 。
       change in the Group’s business operation.
(11)   Core profit before income tax expense is defined as profit before income tax              (11)   除所得稅費用前核心溢利指不包括物業 、廠房及
       expense excluding impairment losses on property, plant and equipment, intangible                 設備 、無形資產 、應收賬款及其他應收款項減值
       assets, accounts and other receivables and exchange gain/loss.                                   虧損及匯兌收益╱虧損之除所得稅費用前溢利 。
(12)   Core profit attributable to shareholders of the Company is defined as profit              (12)   本公司股東應佔核心溢利指不包括本公司股東應
       attributable to shareholders of the Company excluding impairment losses on                       佔之物業 、廠房及設備 、無形資產 、應收賬款及
       property, plant and equipment, intangible assets, accounts and other receivables                 其他應收款項減值虧損及匯兌收益╱虧損及其有
       and exchange gain/loss attributable to shareholders of the Company and its                       關稅務影響之本公司股東應佔溢利 。
       related tax effect.
(13)   Adjusted EBITDA is defined as profit before income tax expense from continuing            (13)   經調整扣除利息 、稅項 、折舊及攤銷前盈利指不
       operations, excluding impairment loss on property, plant and equipment,                          包括物業 、廠房及設備減值虧損 、無形資產減值
       impairment loss on intangible assets, impairment loss on accounts and other                      虧損 、應收賬款及其他應收款項減值虧損 、匯兌
       receivables, exchange gain/loss, interest and depreciation, depletion and                        收益╱虧損 、利息以及折舊 、損耗及攤銷之持續
       amortisation.                                                                                    經營業務除所得稅費用前溢利 。

CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告




                                                                              Fu Bin 付斌
                                                          Chairman and Executive Director
                                                                         主席兼執行董事




14   KUNLUN ENERGY COMPANY LIMITED   Annual Report 2021
                                                                                       CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
                                                                                                   主席報告




The Group will adhere to the market-oriented and benefit-focused
approach to enhance the cooperation in the integrally installed urban
gas projects, actively establish a deep integrated partnership between
natural gas and new energy, and continuously expand the derived
value and transformation space of the industrial chain, so as to
 constantly create value for shareholders, employees, customers
    and the society.

During the Year, the Group seized favorable opportunities such as
strong domestic demand for natural gas and robust rebound in crude
oil prices, and achieved good results in each business segment.
Profit before income tax expense from continuing operations was
approximately RMB10,576 million, representing an increase of
30.46% as compared with RMB8,107 million for the last year.
Profit attributable to shareholders of the Company for the Year
was approximately RMB23,016 million, representing an increase of
279.61% as compared with RMB6,063 million for the last year.
本年度，本集團搶抓國內天然氣需求旺盛、原油價格強力反彈等有利時機，
各業務板塊均取得了良好業績，實現持續經營業務之除所得稅費用前溢利
約為人民幣10,576百萬元，比去年金額人民幣8,107百萬元增加30.46%。於
本年度本公司股東應佔溢利約為人民幣23,016百萬元，比去年金額人民幣
6,063百萬元增加279.61%。

企業管治報告

The board of directors (the “Board”) of Kunlun Energy Company           昆侖能源有限公司（「本公司」及其附屬公司統
Limited (the “Company”, and together with its subsidiaries, the         稱「本集團」）董事會（「董事會」）欣然於截至
“Group”) is pleased to present this Corporate Governance Report in      2021年12月31日止年度（「本年度」）本公司年
the Company’s annual report for the year ended 31 December 2021         報內呈報此份企業管治報告 。
(the “Year”).

The Company recognises the importance of good corporate governance      本公司深明良好企業管治對本公司健全發展
to the Company’s healthy growth and has devoted considerable            之重要性 ，並已致力尋求及制定切合其業務
efforts to identifying and formulating corporate governance practices   需要之企業管治常規 。
appropriate to the needs of its business.

The Company’s corporate governance practices are based on the 本公司之企業管治常規乃建基於香港聯合交
principles (the “Principles”), code provisions (the “Code Provisions”) 易所有限公司（「聯交所」）證券上市規則（「上
and certain recommended best practices (the “Recommended Best 市規則」）附錄14所載企業管治守則（「企業管
Practices”) as set out in the Corporate Governance Code (the “CG 治守則」）內所列之原則（「原則」）、守則條文
Code”) contained in Appendix 14 to the Rules Governing the Listing of （「守則條文」）及若干建議最佳常規（「建議最
Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock 佳常規」）而釐定 。
Exchange”) (the “Listing Rules”).

The Company has applied the Principles and the Code Provisions as       本公司於本年度已應用企業管治守則所載之
set out in the CG Code and complied with all the Code Provisions        原則及守則條文 ，並遵守所有守則條文 。
throughout the Year.

The Company has also put in place certain Recommended Best              本公司亦已實施企業管治守則所載之若干建
Practices as set out in the CG Code.                                    議最佳常規 。

The Company periodically reviews its corporate governance practices     本公司定期檢討其企業管治常規以確保該等
to ensure that these continue to meet the requirements of the CG        常規持續符合企業管治守則之規定 ，並確認
Code, and acknowledges the important role of its Board in providing     其董事會於為本公司業務提供有效領導及方

Practices and Conduct of Meetings                                         會議常規及操守

financial position of the Company and to enable them to make informed     況 ，並能夠在掌握有關資料之情況下作出決
decisions. The Board and each Director also have separate and             定 。需要時 ，董事會及每名董事亦有個別及
independent access to the senior management whenever necessary.           獨立途徑接觸高級管理人員 。

According to current Board practice, any material transaction, which      根據現行董事會常規 ， 任何涉及主要股東或
involves a conflict of interests for a substantial shareholder or a       董事之利益衝突之重大交易 ，將由董事會於
Director, will be considered and dealt with by the Board at a duly        正式召開之董事會會議上作出考慮及處理 。
convened Board meeting. The Company’s Bye-laws also contain               本公司細則亦載有條文 ，規定董事於批准該
provisions requiring Directors to abstain from voting and not to be       等董事或彼等任何聯繫人士有重大利益之交
counted in the quorum at meetings for approving transactions in which     易之會議上放棄投票 ，且不計入會議法定人
such Directors or any of their associates have a material interest.       數。

      inadequate, to modify our opinion. Our conclusions are based              我們應當發表非無保留意見。我們的結

3   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)                       3   主要會計政策（續）
    (b) Associates and joint ventures (Continued)                         (b) 聯營公司及合資企業
                                                                             （續）
        An investment in an associate or a joint venture is                  聯營公司或合資企業之投資乃根
        accounted for in the consolidated financial statements               據權益法在綜合財務報表中列
        using the equity method. Under the equity method, the                賬 。根據權益法 ，投資初始以成
        investment is initially recorded at cost, which includes             本記賬 ，成本包括購買價 、直接
        purchase price, other costs directly attributable to the             來自收購該投資的其他成本 ，及
        acquisition of the investment, and any direct investment             構成本集團股本投資一部分的於
        into the associate or joint venture that forms part of the           聯營公司或合資企業的任何直接
        Group’s equity investment. Thereafter, the investment is             投資 。其後 ，該投資經本集團在
        adjusted for the post acquisition change in the Group’s              收購後應佔被投資單位之淨資產

           from that use.                                                         濟利益 ，則控制權已被轉移 。

                Measurement of ECLs (Continued)                                          計量預期信貸虧損（續）

                In measuring ECLs, the Group takes into account                          於計量預期信貸虧損時 ，本
                reasonable and supportable information that is                           集團考慮合理及有理據而無
                available without undue cost or effort. This includes                    需花費過多的成本或勞力所
                information about past events, current conditions and                    獲得的資料 。這包括過去事
                forecasts of future economic conditions.                                 件 、當前狀況和未來經濟狀
                                                                                         況預測等資料 。

                ECLs are measured on either of the following bases:                      預期信貸虧損基於下列其中
                                                                                         一個基準計量：

                –    12-month ECLs: these are losses that are                            –   12個月的預期信貸虧
                     expected to result from possible default events                         損： 預 計 在 報 告 日 後
                     within the 12 months after the reporting date;                          12個月內可能發生的
                     and                                                                     違約事件而導致的虧
                                                                                             損；及

                –    lifetime ECLs: these are losses that are expected                   –   整個存續期的預期信
                     to result from all possible default events over                         貸 虧 損： 預 計 該 等 採
                     the expected lives of the items to which the                            用預期信貸虧損模式
                     ECL model applies.                                                      的項目在整個存續期
                                                                                             內所有可能發生的違
                                                                                             約事件而導致的虧損。

                Loss allowances for accounts receivable, lease                           應收賬款 、租賃應收款項及
                receivables and contract assets, are always measured                     合約資產的虧損撥備一般是
                at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs. ECLs on these                       以整個存續期的預期信貸虧
                financial assets are estimated using a provision                         損的金額計量 。該等金融資
                matrix based on the Group’s historical credit loss                       產的預期信貸虧損是利用基

    (s) Provisions                                                            (s) 撥備
          Provisions for environmental restoration, restructuring                   如(i)本集團因過往事件須承擔現
          costs and legal claims are recognised when: (i) the Group                 時法定或推定責任；(ii)履行該責
          has a present legal or constructive obligation as a result of             任很有可能導致資源流出；及(iii)
          past events; (ii) it is probable that an outflow of resources             所涉金額能可靠估計時 ，則須確
          will be required to settle the obligation; and (iii) reliable             認環境修復 、重建成本及法律索
          estimates of the amount can be made.                                      償撥備 。

          Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                      如有多項類似的責任 ，履行該等
          likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement is              責任導致資源流出的可能性乃透
          determined by considering the class of obligations as a                   過整體考慮某責任類別釐定 。即
          whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of                使同類別責任中任何一項可能導
          an outflow with respect to any one item included in the                   致資源流出之機會不大 ，但仍會
          same class of obligations may be small.                                   確認撥備 。

          Provisions are measured at the present value of the                       撥備乃採用稅前貼現率按預期履
          expenditure expected to be required to settle the obligation              行該責任所需開支之現值計量 ，
          using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market                該稅前利率反映現時市場對貨幣
          assessments of the time value of money and the risks                      時間價值及該責任之特定風險之
          specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision                 評估 。因時間推移產生之撥備增
          due to passage of time is recognised as interest expenses.                加乃確認為利息支出 。

               upon disposal or when the operation meets the                              持 作 出 售 之 條 件（ 見 上 文 (i)
               criteria to be classified as held-for-sale (see (i) above),                以較早者為準），則分類為
               if earlier. It also occurs if the operation is abandoned.                  已終止持續經營業務 。撤出

                                                                                          已終止持續經營業務 。

               Where an operation is classified as discontinued,                          倘若業務分類為已終止持續

34 SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                      34 板塊資料
      Operating segments are reported in a manner consistent with             營運板塊之呈報方式與向主要營運決策
      the internal reporting provided to the chief operating decision         者提供之內部報告貫徹一致 ，乃由本集
      maker which is determined by the Group's most senior executive          團最高管理層及本公司執行董事決定 。
      management and Executive Directors of the Company.

      The Group organises its business around products and services.          本集團按產品及服務組織其業務 。就產
      From the products and services perspective, the Group is                品及服務而言 ，本集團廣泛從事一系列
      engaged in a broad range of oil and gas related activities and          石油和天然氣相關業務 ，其收入來自四
      derives its revenue from its four operating segments: Natural           個營運板塊：天然氣銷售 、LPG銷售 、
      Gas Sales, Sales of LPG, LNG Processing and Terminal and                LNG加工與儲運及勘探與生產 。
      Exploration and Production.

      The Natural Gas Sales segment is engaged in the retail sales,           天然氣銷售板塊從事不同天然氣產品的
      distribution and trading of various natural gas products. The Sales     零售及分銷與貿易 。LPG銷售板塊從事
      of LPG segment is engaged in the wholesales and retail sales of         不同LPG產品的批發與零售 。LNG加工
      various LPG products. LNG Processing and Terminal segment is            與儲運板塊從事LNG加工 、卸載 、儲
      engaged in the processing, unloading, storage, gasification and         存 、氣化及裝車 。勘探與生產板塊從
      entrucking of LNG. The Exploration and Production segment is            事原油及天然氣之勘探 、開發 、生產
      engaged in the exploration, development, production and sales           和銷售 。
      of crude oil and natural gas.

      The Group's most senior executive management and Executive              本集團最高管理層及執行董事根據各板
      Directors assess the performance of the operating segments              塊之除所得稅費用 、應佔聯營公司及合
      based on each segment’s profit/(loss) before income tax expense,        資企業之溢利減虧損前之溢利╱（虧損）
      and share of profits less losses of associates and joint ventures       評估經營板塊之表現（「板塊業績」）。
      (“segment results”).

      Segment assets exclude deferred and current taxes, financial            板塊資產不包括遞延及即期稅項 、按公

      are managed on a central basis.                                         項均集中管理 。

      expenses incurred at corporate level.                                   用及利息支出 。

Definition:                                                           釋義：
The petroleum reserves are classified as follows:                     石油儲量分類如下：

Proved oil and gas reserves – Proved oil and gas reserves are         探明油氣儲量 －探明油氣儲量為自給定日期
those quantities of oil and gas, which by analysis of geoscience      至合同約定權利到期日（除非有證據合理保證
and engineering data, can be estimated with reasonable certainty      該權利能夠得到延期），透過地球科學和工程
to be economically producible – from a given date forward, from       數據的分析 ，採用確定性或概率性評估 ，以
known reservoirs, and under existing economic conditions, operating   現有經濟 、作業和政府管制條件 ，可以合理
methods, and government regulations – prior to the time at which      確定已知油氣藏經濟可採油氣的數量 。項目
contracts providing the right to operate expire, unless evidence      須已開始萃取烴或作業者須合理確定其將於
indicates that renewal is reasonably certain, regardless of whether   合理時間內開始項目 。
deterministic or probabilistic methods are used for the estimation.
The project to extract the hydrocarbons must have commenced or
the operator must be reasonably certain that it will commence the
project within a reasonable time.

Developed oil and gas reserves – Developed oil and gas reserves               已開發油氣儲量 －已開發油氣儲量為預期可
are reserves of any category that can be expected to be recovered:            透過以下方式開採的儲量：

(i)     Through existing wells with existing equipment and operating          (i)     利用現有設備和作業方法 ，或者開採儲
        methods or in which the cost of the required equipment is                     量所需的開發設備成本明顯低於鑽探一
        relatively minor compared to the cost of a new well; and                      口新井所需成本 ，可從現有油氣井中進
                                                                                      行開採的儲量；及

(ii)    Through installed extraction equipment and infrastructure             (ii)    當通過除油氣井開採外的其他方式進行
        operational at the time of the reserves estimate if the extraction            開採 ，利用儲量估計時點已安裝的開採
        is by means not involving a well.                                             設備和基礎設施可開採的儲量 。

Undeveloped oil and gas reserves – Undeveloped oil and gas reserves           未開發油氣儲量 －未開發油氣儲量指預期可
are reserves of any category that are expected to be recovered from           從未鑽區域的新井中採出 ，或再完井需要較
new wells on undrilled acreage, or from existing wells where a relatively     高支出的現有井中採出的儲量 。
major expenditure is required from recompletion.

(i)     Reserves on undrilled acreage shall be limited to those directly      (i)     未鑽區域的儲量僅限於直接扣除開發間
        offsetting development spacing areas that are reasonably                      距區域 ，有一定把握在鑽後能採油 ，
        certain of production when drilled, unless evidence using                     除非證實運用可靠技術能有一定把握在
        reliable technology exists that establishes reasonable certainty              更遠距離在經濟上可生產 。
        of economic producibility at greater distances.

(ii)    Undrilled locations can be classified as having undeveloped           (ii)    倘已採納的開發方案表明計劃在五年內
        reserves only if a development plan has been adopted indicating               （在特定環境下須較長時間者除外）鑽
        that they are scheduled to be drilled within five years, unless the           井 ，未鑽區域方可分類為擁有未開發儲
        specific circumstances, justify a longer time.                                量。

(iii)   Under no circumstances shall estimates for undeveloped reserves       (iii)   任何未開發儲量的估計均不得包括擬運
        be attributable to any acreage for which an application of fluid              用流體注入或其他改良採油技術的任何
        injection or other improved recovery technique is contemplated,               區域 ，除非該等技術已通過在相同油藏
        unless such techniques have been proved effective by actual                   或類似油藏的實際項目 ，或通過採用可
        projects in the same reservoir or an analogous reservoir, or by               靠技術證實有一定把握的其他證據證明
        other evidence using reliable technology establishing reasonable              為有效 。
        certainty.
